# web\templates\history_row.html
```html
{#<!-- templates/history_row.html -->#}
{% macro render_history_tab(paper, set_num, tab_name, show_certainty) %}
<div class="history-tab-panel {% if tab_name == 'main' %}active{% endif %}" data-tab-panel="{{ tab_name }}" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
    {#<!-- Trace Content -->#}
    <div class="trace-content">
        {% set log_entries = paper['llm_log_entries'] if set_num is none else paper['set_' + (set_num|string) + '_llm_log_entries'] %}
        {% if log_entries %}
            {% for entry in log_entries %}
            <div class="log-entry log-type-{{ entry.type }}">
                <div class="log-header">
                    <span class="log-timestamp">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</span>
                    <span class="log-type">{{ entry.type | capitalize }}{% if set_num %} (Set {{ set_num }}){% endif %}</span>
                    {% if entry.type != 'user' %}
                    <span class="log-model">Model: {{ entry.model }}</span>
                    {% endif %}
                    <span class="log-validity">
                    {% if entry.valid %}
                    <span class="valid-indicator valid">✓ Valid</span>
                    {% else %}
                    <span class="valid-indicator invalid" title="{{ entry.invalid_reason|default('') }}">✗ Invalid</span>
                    {% if entry.invalid_reason %}
                        <span class="invalid-reason">{{ entry.invalid_reason }}</span>
                    {% endif %}
                    {% endif %}
                    </span>
                </div>
                {% if entry.trace %}<div class="log-trace"><pre>{{ entry.trace }}</pre></div>{% endif %}
                {% if entry.verification_data %}
                <div class="log-verification-summary">
                    <strong>Verification:</strong>
                    {% if entry.verification_data.verified == 1 %}✓ Approved{% elif entry.verification_data.verified == 0 %}✗ Rejected{% else %}❔ Unknown{% endif %}
                    {% if entry.verification_data.estimated_score is not none %} | Score: {{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
                </div>
                {% endif %}
            </div>
            {% endfor %}
        {% else %}
            <div class="log-entry log-type-user"><p>No classification data{% if set_num %} for Set {{ set_num }}{% endif %}.</p></div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    {#<!-- Main History Table -->#}
    <div id="history-table-wrapper">
        <table class="history-table">
            <colgroup>
                <col style="width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Off-topic -->#}
                <col style="width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Relevance -->#}
                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                        <col style="width: 27px;">
                    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                        {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                            <col style="width: 27px;">
                        {% endfor %}
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
                <col style="width: 56px;"> {#<!-- Timestamp -->#}
                <col style="width: 40px;"> {#<!-- Changed By -->#}
                <col style="width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Verified -->#}
                <col style="width: 32px;"> {#<!-- Accr. Score -->#}
                <col style="width: 27px;"> {#<!-- Verified By -->#}
            </colgroup>
            <tbody>
                {% for entry in log_entries %}
                    {% if entry.type in ['classifier', 'consensus', 'averaged_llm', 'user'] and entry.valid %}
                    <tr>
                        {% set data = entry.output %}
                        {% set changed = entry.changed_fields %}
                        {% set certainty = entry.certainty_map if show_certainty and entry.certainty_map else {} %}
                        
                        {#<!-- Universal Inferred -->#}
                        <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get('is_offtopic', 'solid') }}{% if 'is_offtopic' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                            {% if certainty.get('is_offtopic') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                            {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data.is_offtopic | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                        </td>
                        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'relevance' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                            {% if data.relevance is defined and data.relevance is not none %}{{ (data.relevance|float|round(1)|string).rstrip('0').rstrip('.') }}{% endif %}
                        </td>
                        
                        {#<!-- Dynamic Domain-Specific -->#}
                        {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                            {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                                {% set path = group.json_path %}
                                <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                    {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                                    {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                                </td>
                            {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                                {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                                    {% set path = group.json_path + '.' + field_def.key %}
                                    {% if field_def.get('render_type') == 'text_presence' %}
                                        <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                            {% set text_val = data | get_json_path(path) %}
                                            <span class="emoji-content">{{ '✔️' if text_val and (text_val|string).strip() else '❌' }}</span>
                                        </td>
                                    {% else %}
                                        <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                            {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                                            {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                                        </td>
                                    {% endif %}
                                {% endfor %}
                            {% endif %}
                        {% endfor %}
                        
                        {#<!-- Trailing Fixed -->#}
                        <td class="secondary-text-cell changed-cell">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</td>
                        <td class="status-cell changed-by-cell">{{ entry.model|render_changed_by }}</td>
                        
                        {#<!-- Verification Fields -->#}
                        {% if entry.type == 'averaged_llm' %}
                            <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get('verified', 'solid') }}{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                {% if certainty.get('verified') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                                {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ data.verified|default(None)|render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                            </td>
                            <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'estimated_score' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                {% if data.estimated_score is not none %}{{ data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
                            </td>
                            <td class="status-cell">
                                {% if data.verified is not none %}🖥️{% else %}❔{% endif %}
                            </td>
                        {% elif entry.type == 'user' %}
                            <td class="status-cell{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                <span class="emoji-content">{{ data.verified|default(None)|render_status }}</span>
                            </td>
                            <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'estimated_score' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                {% if data.estimated_score is not none %}{{ data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
                            </td>
                            <td class="status-cell">{{ data.verified_by|default('')|render_verified_by }}</td>
                        {% elif entry.verification_data %}
                            <td class="status-cell{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                {{ entry.verification_data.verified|render_status }}
                            </td>
                            <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'estimated_score' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">
                                {% if entry.verification_data.estimated_score is not none %}{{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
                            </td>
                            <td class="status-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.verifier_model|render_verified_by }}</td>
                        {% else %}
                            <td class="status-cell">❔</td>
                            <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell"></td>
                            <td class="status-cell">❔</td>
                        {% endif %}
                    </tr>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
        </table>
    </div>

    {% if tab_name == 'main' %}
    <div class="history-info">
        <div style="text-align:center">
            <span>History sorted from <b>newest</b> to <b>oldest</b>.</span><br><br>
            <span>Highlights identify cells changed between runs.</span><br>
            <span>⚠️ indicates conflicting classifications across the 3 sets.</span><br>
            <span>Translucent emojis indicate partial agreement (33% = 1 set, 66% = 2 sets agree).</span>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="row-actions">
        <div>
            <button type="button" class="action-btn classify-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">Classify <strong>this paper</strong></button>
            <button type="button" class="action-btn verify-btn" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">Verify <strong>this paper</strong></button>
        </div>
    </div>
    {% endif %}
</div>
{% endmacro %}

{% block history_row_content %}

    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}

        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
    {% endif %}
{% endfor %}

    {#<!-- 4 Vertical Tabs Navigation -->#}
```

# web\templates\papers_table.html

```html
{#<!-- templates/papers_table.html -->#}
{% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
{% for group in domain_config.groups %}
    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
    {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
{% set total_cols = 6 + 2 + ns.total_dynamic + 9 %}

<tbody>
    <tr>
        <td style="padding:0;" colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
            <div class="loading-overlay">
                <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-ui-color);">Parça</span></div>
                <div class="loading-spinner"></div>
                <div>Reprocessing table. Wait patiently. </div>
            </div>
        </td>
        <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
    </tr>

    {% for paper in papers %}
    {% set c = paper.classification %}
    {% set certainty = paper.main_certainty %}
    <tr data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
        {#<!-- 1. LEADING FIXED (6) -->#}
        <td class="status-cell pdf-status emoji-column">
            {% if paper.pdf_filename %}
            <a href="{{ url_for('static', filename='pdfjs/web/viewer.html') }}?file={{ url_for('files.serve_pdf', paper_id=paper.id) | urlencode }}" target="_blank" class="pdf-link"
               title="{% if paper.pdf_state == 'annotated' %}Open this annotated PDF in the Annotator{% else %}Open this PDF in the Annotator{% endif %}">
                {{ pdf_emojis.get(paper.pdf_state) }}
            </a>
            {% else %}
            <a href="#" class="pdf-upload-link" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}"
               title="{% if paper.pdf_state == 'paywalled' %}Article is paywalled. Click to upload if a copy is available{% else %}No PDF stored yet. Click to upload PDF for this article{% endif %}">
                {{ pdf_emojis.get(paper.pdf_state) }}
            </a>
            {% endif %}
        </td>
        <td class="title-cell">
            {% if paper.doi %}<a href="https://doi.org/{{ paper.doi }}" target="_blank">{{ paper.title }}</a>
            {% else %}<span style="font-weight: 300;">{{paper.title}}</span>{% endif %}
        </td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell">{{ paper.year or '' }}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="page_count">{{ paper.page_count or '' }}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell">{{ paper.deannualized_conference or paper.journal }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell emoji-column" title="{{ paper.type }}">{{ type_emojis.get(paper.type, default_type_emoji) }}</td>

        {#<!-- 2. UNIVERSAL INFERRED (2) -->#}
        <td class="status-cell editable-status certainty-{{ certainty.get('is_offtopic', 'solid') }}" data-field="is_offtopic">
            {% if certainty.get('is_offtopic') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
            {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ c.get('is_offtopic') | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
        </td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="relevance">{{ (c.get('relevance')|round(1)|string).rstrip('0').rstrip('.') if c.get('relevance') else '' }}</td>
        
        {#<!-- 3. DYNAMIC DOMAIN-SPECIFIC -->#}
        {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
            {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                {% set path = group.json_path %}
                <td class="status-cell editable-status certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}" data-field="{{ path }}">
                    {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                    {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ c | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                </td>
            {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                    {% set path = group.json_path + '.' + field_def.key %}
                    {% set highlight_class = 'highlight-status' if field_def.get('highlight') else '' %}
                    
                    {% if field_def.get('render_type') == 'text_presence' %}
                        <td class="status-cell {{ highlight_class }} certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}" data-field="{{ path }}">
                            {% set text_val = c | get_json_path(path) %}
                            <span class="emoji-content">{{ '✔️' if text_val and (text_val|string).strip() else '❌' }}</span>
                        </td>
                    {% else %}
                        <td class="status-cell editable-status {{ highlight_class }} certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}" data-field="{{ path }}">
                            {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
                            {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ c | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                        </td>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
            {% endif %}
        {% endfor %}

        {#<!-- 4. TRAILING FIXED (9) -->#}
        <td class="secondary-text-cell changed-cell highlight-status">{{ paper.changed_formatted }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell changed-by-cell highlight-status" data-field="changed_by">{{ paper.changed_by | render_changed_by }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell editable-status certainty-{{ certainty.get('verified', 'solid') }}" data-field="verified">
            {% if certainty.get('verified') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>
            {% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ paper.verified | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
        </td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="estimated_score">{{ paper.estimated_score or '' }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell editable-verify" data-field="verified_by">{{ paper.verified_by | render_verified_by }}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="user_override_count">{{ paper.user_override_count or '0' }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell" data-field="user_comment_state">{{ '✔️' if paper.user_trace and (paper.user_trace|string).strip() else '❌' }}</td>
        <td class="toggle-btn history-btn" onclick="toggleHistory(this)"><span>Show</span><br><span class="arrow">▼</span></td>
        <td class="toggle-btn" onclick="toggleDetails(this)"><span>Show</span><br><span class="arrow">▼</span></td>
        
        {#<!-- Hidden data cells for search indexing -->#}
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="abstract" style="display: none;">{{ paper.abstract }}</td>
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="keywords" style="display: none;">{{ paper.keywords }}</td>
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="authors" style="display: none;">{{ paper.authors }}</td>
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="user_trace" style="display: none;">{{ paper.user_trace or '' }}</td>
            
    {#<!-- Dynamic Domain-Specific Editable Fields (Restored for Search) -->#}
    {% for field in domain_config.editable_fields %}
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="{{ field.json_path | replace('.', '_') }}" style="display: none;">
            {{ paper.classification | get_json_path(field.json_path) or '' }}
        </td>
    {% endfor %}
    </tr>
    
    {#<!-- Detail Row Placeholder -->#}
    <tr class="detail-row">
        <td colspan="{{ total_cols }}" class="detail-content-placeholder"></td>
        <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
    </tr>
    {#<!-- History Row Placeholder -->#}
    <tr class="history-row">
        <td colspan="{{ total_cols }}" class="detail-content-placeholder"></td>
        <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
    </tr>
    {% endfor %}
</tbody>
```

# web\templates\about_modal_content.html
```html
<!-- templates/about_modal_content.html -->
<div class="modal-small-section">
    <h3><strong>Quick Help</strong> Guide</h3>
    <p style="margin-bottom: 0"><strong>Understanding the Symbols:</strong></p>
    <ul style="list-style-type: none;  padding-left: 0;  margin: 0; column-count: 3; gap: 0">
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table" style="opacity:0.33">✔️</strong>: 1/3 sets agree on "Yes";</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table" style="opacity:0.66">✔️</strong>: 2/3 sets agree on "Yes";</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">✔️</strong>: Full agreement on "Yes";</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">❔</strong>: Unknown / Not Classified / Not Applicable;</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">⚠️</strong>: <strong>Conflict</strong> (sets disagree Yes/No);</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table" style="opacity:0.33">❌</strong>: 1/3 sets agree on "No";</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table" style="opacity:0.66">❌</strong>: 2/3 sets agree on "No";</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">❌</strong>: Full agreement on "No";</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">👤</strong>: Last changed or verified by the user;</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">🖥️</strong>: Last changed or verified by AI Model;</p></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">📕</strong>: A PDF is attached to this paper;</li></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">📗</strong>: An <strong>annotated</strong> PDF is attached to this paper;</li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">💰</strong>: The PDF for this paper is <strong>paywalled</strong></li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">📄</strong>: This is a <strong>Journal</strong> paper</li>
        <li><p style="margin: 0"><strong class="about-emoji-table">📚</strong>: This is a <strong>Conference</strong> paper</li>
    </ul>
    <p><strong>How to Use:</strong></p>
    <div id="help-text">
        <p><strong>Filter Papers:</strong> Use the year range, minimum page count, and the search bar at the top. Press
            "Apply" (appears on change) for server-side filters.</p>
        <p><strong>Checkbox filters:</strong> Use checkboxes like "Hide Offtopic", "Hide X-Ray", or "Only Survey" for quick
            client-side filtering. As the checkboxes have no permanent labels, hover each checkbox to identify the purpose
            for each. Survey, THT, SMT, and Dataset checkboxes cycle through three states (All → Only Yes → Only No) using
            standard checked, unchecked, and indeterminate visuals.</p>
        <p><strong>Certainty Indicators:</strong> Classification results are averaged across three independent runs. Emojis
            with partial translucency indicate partial agreement (33% or 66% opacity). A <strong
                class="about-emoji-text">⚠️</strong> warning icon appears when runs directly conflict (e.g., two "Yes" vs
            one "No"). Fully solid emojis mean all three runs agree completely.</p>
        <p><strong>Classify/Verify:</strong> Click the "Classify this paper" or "Verify this paper" button in the detail row
            to run LLM-based classification on a single paper. Use the top buttons in the "Batch Actions" menu to classify
            or verify<em>remaining</em> or <em>all</em> papers.</p>
        <p><strong>Edit Data:</strong> Click on any <strong class="about-emoji-text">✔️ ❌ ❔</strong> symbol in the main
            table to cycle its value (this is automatically saved on change). Click 'show' in the details column to edit
            text fields (like "Model Name" or "Other Defects").
            These have to be saved manually by clicking on "Save Changes". A paper can be marked as paywalled by including "paywalled" in the user comment <em>Unavailable inside HTML exports.</em></p>
        <p><strong>Tracking User Edits:</strong> The "User Changes" column displays a count of fields you have manually
            overridden compared to the last LLM classification, helping you quickly identify papers that may need
            re-evaluation.</p>
        <p><strong>Search:</strong> Type on the search bar for instant search on all relevant fields. User comments are
            searchable. Classifier/Verifier traces are not (to avoid false-positives/noise)</p>
        <p><strong>View Statistics:</strong> Click the "View Statistics" button to see charts and lists of repeating
            journals, authors, keywords, and mentioned features/models as well as relevant charts.
            The stats are calculated based on currently visible filtered data.</p>
        <p><strong>History & Classification Sets:</strong> Opening a paper's history reveals four vertical tabs. The
            <strong>Main</strong> tab shows the averaged consensus, certainty overlays, and user edits. The <strong>Set
                1</strong>, <strong>Set 2</strong>, and <strong>Set 3</strong> tabs display the raw, individual logs for
            each of the three parallel classification runs, allowing you to inspect exactly where disagreements occurred.
        </p>
        <p><strong>Export and Backup:</strong> Use the "Export HTML" or "Export XLSX" buttons inside the "Export & Backup"
            menu to download the currently filtered dataset,
            or use the backup / restore buttons to work woith full <em>*.parça.zst</em> backups.
            Backups include the database, original and annotated PDFs, HTML export and a XLSX spreadsheet. <em>Unavailable
                inside HTML exports.</em>
        </p>
        <p><strong>Import Data:</strong> Use the "Import BibTeX" button to add new papers to the database from a .bib file.
            <em>Unavailable inside HTML exports.</em></p>
        <p><strong>Attach PDF:</strong> Click on the <strong class="about-emoji-text">❔</strong> in the PDF column of a
            paper to upload a PDF to it. <em>Unavailable inside HTML exports.</em></p>
        <p><strong>Read/Annotate PDF:</strong> Click on the <strong class="about-emoji-text">📕</strong> or <strong
                class="about-emoji-text">📗</strong> of an existing PDF to open the integrated PDF.js annotator.
            All annotations are autosaved after 5 seconds. The annotated status only changes from <strong
                class="about-emoji-text">📕</strong> to <strong class="about-emoji-text">📗</strong> after a page reload.
            This doesn't affect annotation saving.
            For the HTML export, the PDF is loaded directly (read-only) on the user's default reader instead. This requires
            the PDF to be in the correct folder structure, as obtained from a full backup.</p>
        <p><strong>State Preservation:</strong> Your active filters, sorting preferences, and which detail or history rows
            are expanded are automatically preserved in the browser's URL. This allows you to bookmark or share a specific
            view of the table, which will restore exactly as you left it upon reload.</p>
        <p><strong>Tip:</strong> The table is pre-sorted to show papers with user comments first. You can click any column
            header to sort by that field.</p>
    </div>
</div>
<div class="modal-small-section" style="background: var(--detail-button-background);">
    <h3><strong>About</strong></h3>
    <div class="header-branding" style="padding: 10px 0 0;  text-align: center;"><span>Research</span><span
            style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-ui-color);">Parça</span></div>
    <p><strong><a href="https://github.com/Zidrewndacht/bibtex-custom-parser" target="_blank"
                rel="noopener">ReseachParça</a> - PCB Inspection Papers Database Browser.</a></strong></p>
    <p>Developed by <a href="https://zidrewndacht.github.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><strong>Luis Alfredo da
                Silva</strong></a>,
        to assist his own Master's degree research at <strong><a href="https://www.udesc.br/cct" target="_blank"
                rel="noopener">Universidade do Estado de Santa Catarina <strong>(UDESC)</strong></a></strong>. </p>
    <p>The following open source libraries, and its dependencies, are leveraged by this software:</p>
    <p><strong>Backend:</strong>
        <a href="https://www.python.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Python</a>,
        <a href="https://flask.palletsprojects.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Flask</a>,
        <a href="https://www.sqlite.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">SQLite3</a>
    </p>
    <p><strong>Frontend:</strong>
        <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Guide/HTML/HTML5" target="_blank"
            rel="noopener">HTML5</a>,
        <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS" target="_blank" rel="noopener">CSS3</a>,
        <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Vanilla JS</a>
    </p>
    <p><strong>Data Visualization:</strong>
        <a href="https://www.chartjs.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Chart.js</a>
    </p>
    <p><strong>PDF View/Annotation/Autosave:</strong>
        <a href="https://mozilla.github.io/pdf.js/getting_started/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">PDF.js</a>
    </p>
    <p><strong>Utilities:</strong>
        <a href="https://opensource.perlig.de/rcssmin/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">rcssmin</a>,
        <a href="https://opensource.perlig.de/rjsmin/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">rjsmin</a> (minification),
        <a href="https://openpyxl.readthedocs.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openpyxl</a> (Excel export),
        <a href="https://nodeca.github.io/pako/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">pako</a> (compressed HTML export),
        <a href="https://github.com/facebook/zstd" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Zstandard</a> (backup/restore).
    </p>
    <p>Classification and verification was inferenced by Unsloth's Dynamic quants of <strong><a
                href="https://huggingface.co/unsloth/Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507-GGUF" target="_blank"
                rel="noopener">Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507</a></strong> model from <strong><a
                href="https://www.alibabacloud.com/en/solutions/generative-ai/qwen?_p_lc=1" target="_blank"
                rel="noopener">Qwen/Alibaba Cloud</a>.</strong></p>
    <p><strong>Local Inference engine:</strong> <a href="https://hub.docker.com/r/vllm/vllm-openai/" target="_blank"
            rel="noopener">vLLM</a>.</p>
    <p>Original papers data (BibTeX) was sourced from
        <a href="https://www.scopus.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Scopus</a>,
        <a href="https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">IEEE Xplore</a> (some) and
        <a href="https://dl.acm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">ACM Digital Library</a>
    </p>
    <p><strong>Original search query (Scopus format):</strong>
        <span style="font-size:0.75em">TITLE-ABS-KEY(( "printed circuit*" OR "Circuit board*" OR pcb OR pcba ) AND (
            inspection OR manufactur* OR assembly OR defect* OR solder* OR weld* OR "Automat* optical" )) AND PUBYEAR >
            1973 AND PUBYEAR < 2027 AND ( LIMIT-TO ( DOCTYPE,"ar" ) OR LIMIT-TO ( DOCTYPE,"cp" ) OR LIMIT-TO (
                DOCTYPE,"re" ) ) AND ( LIMIT-TO ( PUBSTAGE,"final" ) ) </span>
    </p>
</div>
```

# web\templates\static_export\loader.html
```html
<!-- templates/loader.html -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Loading ResearchParça...</title>    
    <link href="data:image/x-icon;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAABAAAAAQEAYAAABPYyMiAAAABmJLR0T///////8JWPfcAAAACXBIWXMAAABIAAAASABGyWs+AAAAF0lEQVRIx2NgGAWjYBSMglEwCkbBSAcACBAAAeaR9cIAAAAASUVORK5CYII=" rel="icon" type="image/x-icon" />

    <style>
        /* Minimal styling for the loading indicator */
        body { font-family: sans-serif; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 40px; justify-content: center; align-items: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; background-color: #f5f5f5; }
        .loading, #previewMessage { text-align: center; }
        /* .spinner { border: 4px solid #f3f3f3; border-top: 4px solid #3498db; border-radius: 50%; width: 30px; height: 30px; animation: spin 1s linear infinite; margin: 0 auto 10px; } */    
        .spinner {
            width: 60px;
            height: 60px;
            border: 12px solid hsl(188, 36%, 30%);
            border-top: 12px solid transparent;
            border-radius: 50%;
            animation: spin 0.75s linear infinite;
            margin: 0 auto 30px; 
        }
        .header-branding{
            font-size: 48pt;
            justify-self: center;
            font-family: "Arial Narrow";
            font-weight: 400;
            color: #3f8d82;
            text-shadow: 1px 0 5px #fff6;
        }
        @keyframes spin { 0% { transform: rotate(0deg); } 100% { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
    </style>
    <!-- Inline the Pako library for Gzip decompression -->
    <script>
    {{ pako_js_content | safe }} 
    </script>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: hsl(188, 31%, 29%);">Parça</span></div>
    <div class="loading" id="splash-spinner">
        <div class="spinner"></div>
        <p>Trying to decompress and load HTML static export...</p>
        <p>This file requires JavaScript to decompress and load its content.<br>
        Download and open it in a full (computer) web browser to view.</p>
        <!-- Warning apparently not needed anymore in latest Edge/Chromium -->
        <!-- <p><strong>This application has known performance issues in<br>
            Google Chrome / MS Edge.</strong>
        <p>Please use Mozilla Firefox for the best experience. -->
        </p>
    </div>
    <script>
        const compressedDataBase64 = "{{ compressed_html_data | safe }}"; // The compressed HTML data passed from Flask

        function loadContent() {
            try {
                const compressedBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(compressedDataBase64), c => c.charCodeAt(0));  // 1. Decode Base64 to get compressed bytes (as Uint8Array)
                const decompressedBytes = pako.inflate(compressedBytes);                                    // 2. Decompress using Pako (inflate)                
                const decompressedHtmlString = new TextDecoder("utf-8").decode(decompressedBytes);          // 3. Decode bytes back to string (assuming UTF-8)

                // 4. Write the full HTML content to the document, replacing this loader
                // Using document.write after the page loads replaces the entire document
                document.open();
                document.write(decompressedHtmlString);
                document.close();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Failed to decompress or load content:", error);
                document.body.innerHTML = "<p style='color:red;'>Error loading data. Please check the console for details.</p>";
            }
        }

        // Start loading when the window loads
        window.addEventListener('load', loadContent);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

# web\templates\static_export\papers_table_static_export.html
```html
{% block papers_table_static_export %}
{% set ns = namespace(total_dynamic=0) %}
{% for group in domain_config.groups %}
    {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + 1 %}
    {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
        {% set ns.total_dynamic = ns.total_dynamic + (group.fields | length) %}
    {% endif %}
{% endfor %}
{% set inferred_colspan = 2 + ns.total_dynamic %}
{% set total_cols = 6 + inferred_colspan + 9 %}
{# 378px is the width of the fixed contents #}
{% set history_table_width = 378 + ns.total_dynamic * 27 %}

<tbody style="position: relative;">
    <tr>
        <td style="padding:0;" colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
            <div class="loading-overlay">
                <div class="header-branding"><span>Research</span><span style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--primary-ui-color);">Parça</span></div>
                <div class="loading-spinner"></div>
                <div>Reprocessing table. Wait patiently. </div>
            </div>
        </td>
        <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
    </tr>
    {% for paper in papers %}
    {% set c = paper.classification %}
    {% set certainty = paper.main_certainty %}
    <tr data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
        <td class="status-cell pdf-status emoji-column">
        {% if paper.pdf_filename %}
            {% if paper.pdf_state == 'annotated' %}
            <a href="data/pdf_annotated/{{ paper.pdf_filename }}" target="_blank" class="pdf-link" title="Open PDF">
                {{ pdf_emojis.get(paper.pdf_state) }}
            </a>
            {% else %}
            <a href="data/pdf/{{ paper.pdf_filename }}" target="_blank" class="pdf-link" title="Open PDF">
                {{ pdf_emojis.get(paper.pdf_state) }}
            </a>
            {% endif %}
        {% elif paper.pdf_state == 'paywalled' %}
            <span title="This paper is paywalled">
                {{ pdf_emojis.get(paper.pdf_state) }}
            </span>
        {% endif %}
        </td>
        <td class="title-cell">{% if paper.doi %}<a href="https://doi.org/{{ paper.doi }}" target="_blank">{{ paper.title }}</a>{% else %}<span style="font-weight: 300;">{{paper.title}}</span>{% endif %}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell">{{ paper.year or '' }}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="page_count">{{ paper.page_count or '' }}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell">{{ paper.deannualized_conference or paper.journal }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell emoji-column" title="{{ paper.type }}">{{ type_emojis.get(paper.type, default_type_emoji) }}</td>
        
        <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get('is_offtopic', 'solid') }}" data-field="is_offtopic">
            {% if certainty.get('is_offtopic') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>{% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ c.get('is_offtopic') | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
        </td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="relevance">{{ (c.get('relevance')|round(1)|string).rstrip('0').rstrip('.') if c.get('relevance') else '' }}</td>
        
        {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
            {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                {% set path = group.json_path %}
                <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}" data-field="{{ path }}">
                    {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>{% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ c | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                </td>
            {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                    {% set path = group.json_path + '.' + field_def.key %}
                    {% set highlight_class = 'highlight-status' if field_def.get('highlight') else '' %}
                    {% if field_def.get('render_type') == 'text_presence' %}
                        {% set text_val = c | get_json_path(path) %}
                        <td class="status-cell {{ highlight_class }} certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}" data-field="{{ path }}">
                            {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>{% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ '✔️' if text_val and (text_val|string).strip() else '❌' }}</span>{% endif %}
                        </td>
                    {% else %}
                        <td class="status-cell {{ highlight_class }} certainty-{{ certainty.get(path, 'solid') }}" data-field="{{ path }}">
                            {% if certainty.get(path) == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>{% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ c | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}
                        </td>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
            {% endif %}
        {% endfor %}
        
        <td class="secondary-text-cell changed-cell highlight-status">{{ paper.changed_formatted }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell changed-by-cell highlight-status" data-field="changed_by">{{ paper.changed_by | render_changed_by }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ certainty.get('verified', 'solid') }}" data-field="verified">{% if certainty.get('verified') == 'conflict' %}<span class="conflict-warning">⚠️</span>{% else %}<span class="emoji-content">{{ paper.verified | render_status }}</span>{% endif %}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="estimated_score">{{ paper.estimated_score or '' }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell editable-verify" data-field="verified_by">{{ paper.verified_by | render_verified_by }}</td>
        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell" data-field="user_override_count">{{ paper.user_override_count or '0' }}</td>
        <td class="status-cell" data-field="user_comment_state">{{ '✔️' if paper.user_trace and (paper.user_trace|string).strip() else '❌' }}</td>
        <td class="toggle-btn history-btn" onclick="toggleHistory(this)"><span>Show</span><br><span class="arrow">▼</span></td>
        <td class="toggle-btn" onclick="toggleDetails(this)"><span>Show</span><br><span class="arrow">▼</span></td>
        
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="abstract" style="display: none;">{{ paper.abstract }}</td>
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="keywords" style="display: none;">{{ paper.keywords }}</td>
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="authors" style="display: none;">{{ paper.authors }}</td>
        <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="user_trace" style="display: none;">{{ paper.user_trace or '' }}</td>
        {% for field in domain_config.editable_fields %}
            <td class="hidden-data-cell" data-field="{{ field.json_path | replace('.', '_') }}" style="display: none;">{{ paper.classification | get_json_path(field.json_path) or '' }}</td>
        {% endfor %}
    </tr>
    
    <tr class="detail-row">
        <td colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
            <div class="detail-flex-container">
                <div class="detail-content detail-abstract">
                    <div class="id-section">
                        <strong>ID:</strong> {{ paper.id }}
                        <button type="button" class="id-cite-btn" onclick="copyPaperId('{{ paper.id }}', this, 'cite')"><em>\cite</em></button>
                        <button type="button" class="id-citen-btn" onclick="copyPaperId('{{ paper.id }}', this, 'citen')"><em>\citeN</em></button>
                        <button type="button" class="id-copy-btn" onclick="copyPaperId('{{ paper.id }}', this, 'raw')">Copy</button>
                    </div>
                    <p><strong>Abstract:</strong> {{ paper.abstract }}</p>
                </div>
                <div class="detail-content detail-metadata">
                    <div class="bibtex-section">
                        <strong>BibTeX Citation:</strong>
                        <button type="button" class="bibtex-copy-btn" onclick="copyBibtex(`{{ paper | bibtex }}`, this)">Copy</button>
                        <pre class="bibtex-pre">{{ paper | bibtex }}</pre>
                    </div>
                    <div class="searchable-section">
                        <span style="font-size:0.8em; color: var(--light-colored-btn-text);">Click on any keyword or author below to <strong>search</strong> for it:</span>
                        <p><strong>Keywords:</strong>
                            <span class="clickable-keywords">
                                {% if paper.keywords %}
                                    {% for kw in paper.keywords.split(';') %}
                                        {% if kw.strip() %}<span class="clickable-item" data-search-field="keywords" data-search-term="{{ kw.strip() }}">{{ kw.strip() }};</span>{% endif %}
                                    {% endfor %}
                                {% endif %}
                            </span>
                        </p>
                        <p><strong>Authors:</strong>
                            <span class="clickable-authors">
                                {% if paper.authors %}
                                    {% for auth in paper.authors.split(';') %}
                                        {% if auth.strip() %}<span class="clickable-item" data-search-field="authors" data-search-term="{{ auth.strip() }}">{{ auth.strip() }};</span>{% endif %}
                                    {% endfor %}
                                {% endif %}
                            </span>
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <p>
                        <strong>DOI:</strong> {% if paper.doi %}<a href="https://doi.org/{{ paper.doi }}" target="_blank">{{ paper.doi }}</a>{% endif %}<br>
                        <strong> ISSN:</strong> {{ paper.issn or 'None' }}
                        <strong> — Page Range/Start:</strong> {{ paper.pages }}
                    </p>
                </div>
                <div class="edit-section detail-edit">
                    <form id="form-{{ paper.id }}" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
                        {% for field in domain_config.editable_fields %}
                            <label>{{ field.label }}: <input type="text" class="editable" name="{{ field.json_path }}" value="{{ paper.classification | get_json_path(field.json_path) or '' }}" disabled></label>
                        {% endfor %}
                        <label>Page count: <input type="text" class="editable" name="page_count" value="{{ paper.page_count or '' }}" disabled></label>
                        <label>Relevance: <input type="text" class="editable" name="relevance" value="{{ c.get('relevance') or '' }}" placeholder="0 - 10" disabled></label>
                        <label>User comments: <textarea class="editable" name="user_trace" disabled>{{ paper.user_trace or '' }}</textarea></label>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </div>
        </td>
        <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
    </tr>
    
    <tr class="history-row">
        <td colspan="{{ total_cols }}">
            <div class="history-flex-container" style="--history-table-width: {{ history_table_width }}px;">
                <div class="history-tabs-nav">
                    <button class="history-tab-btn active" data-tab="main" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}" onclick="switchHistoryTab(this)"><span>Main <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
                    <button class="history-tab-btn" data-tab="set1" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}" onclick="switchHistoryTab(this)"><span>Set 1 <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
                    <button class="history-tab-btn" data-tab="set2" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}" onclick="switchHistoryTab(this)"><span>Set 2 <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
                    <button class="history-tab-btn" data-tab="set3" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}" onclick="switchHistoryTab(this)"><span>Set 3 <span class="arrow">▼</span></span></button>
                </div>
                <div class="history-tabs-content">
                    <!-- MAIN TAB -->
                    <div class="history-tab-panel active" data-tab-panel="main" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
                        <div class="trace-content">
                            {% if paper.llm_log_entries %}
                                {% for entry in paper.llm_log_entries %}
                                    <div class="log-entry log-type-{{ entry.type }}">
                                        <div class="log-header">
                                            <span class="log-timestamp">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</span>
                                            <span class="log-type">{{ entry.type | capitalize }}</span>
                                            {% if entry.type != 'user' %}<span class="log-model">Model: {{ entry.model }}</span>{% endif %}
                                            <span class="log-validity">
                                                {% if entry.valid %}<span class="valid-indicator valid">✓ Valid</span>{% else %}<span class="valid-indicator invalid">✗ Invalid</span>{% endif %}
                                            </span>
                                        </div>
                                        {% if entry.trace %}<div class="log-trace"><pre>{% if is_lite_export %}Thinking trace unavailable in lite export{% else %}{{ entry.trace }}{% endif %}</pre></div>{% endif %}
                                        {% if entry.verification_data %}
                                            <div class="log-verification-summary">
                                                <strong>Verification:</strong>
                                                {% if entry.verification_data.verified == 1 %}✓ Approved{% elif entry.verification_data.verified == 0 %}✗ Rejected{% else %}❔ Unknown{% endif %}
                                                {% if entry.verification_data.estimated_score is not none %} | Score: {{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score }}{% endif %}
                                            </div>
                                        {% endif %}
                                    </div>
                                {% endfor %}
                            {% else %}
                                <div class="log-entry log-type-user"><p>No classification data.</p></div>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                        <table class="history-table">
                            <colgroup>
                                <col style="width: 27px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
                                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}{% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}<col style="width: 27px;">{% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}{% for f in group.fields %}<col style="width: 27px;">{% endfor %}{% endif %}{% endfor %}
                                <col style="width: 56px;"><col style="width: 40px;"><col style="width: 27px;"><col style="width: 32px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
                            </colgroup>
                            <tbody>
                                {% for entry in paper.llm_log_entries %}
                                {% if entry.type in ['classifier', 'consensus', 'averaged_llm', 'user'] and entry.valid %}
                                <tr>
                                    {% set data = entry.output %}{% set changed = entry.changed_fields %}{% set cert = entry.certainty_map if entry.certainty_map else {} %}
                                    <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get('is_offtopic', 'solid') }}{% if 'is_offtopic' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{% if cert.get('is_offtopic') == 'conflict' %}⚠️{% else %}{{ data.is_offtopic | render_status }}{% endif %}</td>
                                    <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'relevance' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{{ (data.relevance|float|round(1)|string).rstrip('0').rstrip('.') if data.relevance is defined and data.relevance is not none else '' }}</td>
                                    
                                    {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                                        {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                                            {% set path = group.json_path %}
                                            <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{% if cert.get(path) == 'conflict' %}⚠️{% else %}{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}{% endif %}</td>
                                        {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                                            {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                                                {% set path = group.json_path + '.' + field_def.key %}
                                                {% if field_def.get('render_type') == 'text_presence' %}
                                                    {% set text_val = data | get_json_path(path) %}
                                                    <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{% if cert.get(path) == 'conflict' %}⚠️{% else %}{{ '✔️' if text_val and (text_val|string).strip() else '❌' }}{% endif %}</td>
                                                {% else %}
                                                    <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get(path, 'solid') }}{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{% if cert.get(path) == 'conflict' %}⚠️{% else %}{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}{% endif %}</td>
                                                {% endif %}
                                            {% endfor %}
                                        {% endif %}
                                    {% endfor %}
                                    
                                    <td class="secondary-text-cell changed-cell">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</td>
                                    <td class="status-cell changed-by-cell">{{ entry.model|render_changed_by }}</td>
                                    {% if entry.type == 'averaged_llm' %}
                                        <td class="status-cell certainty-{{ cert.get('verified', 'solid') }}{% if 'verified' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{% if cert.get('verified') == 'conflict' %}⚠️{% else %}{{ data.verified|default(None)|render_status }}{% endif %}</td>
                                        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell">{{ data.estimated_score|default('') }}</td><td class="status-cell">🖥️</td>
                                    {% elif entry.verification_data %}
                                        <td class="status-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.verified|render_status }}</td>
                                        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score|default('') }}</td>
                                        <td class="status-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.verifier_model|render_verified_by }}</td>
                                    {% else %}
                                        <td class="status-cell">❔</td><td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell"></td><td class="status-cell">❔</td>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </tr>
                                {% endif %}
                                {% endfor %}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                        <div class="history-info">
                            <div style="text-align:center">
                                <span>History sorted from <b>newest</b> to <b>oldest</b>.</span><br><br>
                                <span>Highlights identify cells changed between runs.</span><br>
                                <span>⚠️ indicates conflicting classifications across the 3 sets.</span><br>
                                <span>Translucent emojis indicate partial agreement (33% = 1 set, 66% = 2 sets agree).</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- SET 1, 2, 3 TABS -->
                    {% for set_num in [1, 2, 3] %}
                    <div class="history-tab-panel" data-tab-panel="set{{ set_num }}" data-paper-id="{{ paper.id }}">
                        <div class="trace-content">
                            {% if paper['set_' ~ set_num ~ '_llm_log_entries'] %}
                                {% for entry in paper['set_' ~ set_num ~ '_llm_log_entries'] %}
                                    <div class="log-entry log-type-{{ entry.type }}">
                                        <div class="log-header">
                                            <span class="log-timestamp">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</span>
                                            <span class="log-type">{{ entry.type | capitalize }} (Set {{ set_num }})</span>
                                            {% if entry.type != 'user' %}<span class="log-model">Model: {{ entry.model }}</span>{% endif %}
                                        </div>
                                        {% if entry.trace %}<div class="log-trace"><pre>{% if is_lite_export %}Thinking trace unavailable in lite export{% else %}{{ entry.trace }}{% endif %}</pre></div>{% endif %}
                                    </div>
                                {% endfor %}
                            {% else %}
                                <div class="log-entry log-type-user"><p>No classification data for Set {{ set_num }}.</p></div>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                        <table class="history-table">
                            <colgroup>
                                <col style="width: 27px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
                                {% for group in domain_config.groups %}{% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}<col style="width: 27px;">{% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}{% for f in group.fields %}<col style="width: 27px;">{% endfor %}{% endif %}{% endfor %}
                                <col style="width: 56px;"><col style="width: 40px;"><col style="width: 27px;"><col style="width: 32px;"><col style="width: 27px;">
                            </colgroup>
                            <tbody>
                                {% for entry in paper['set_' ~ set_num ~ '_llm_log_entries'] %}
                                {% if entry.type in ['classifier', 'consensus', 'user'] and entry.valid %}
                                <tr>
                                    {% set data = entry.output %}{% set changed = entry.changed_fields %}
                                    <td class="status-cell{% if 'is_offtopic' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{{ data.is_offtopic | render_status }}</td>
                                    <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell{% if 'relevance' in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{{ data.relevance if data.relevance is not none else '' }}</td>
                                    
                                    {% for group in domain_config.groups %}
                                        {% if group.filter_type == 'tri_state' %}
                                            {% set path = group.json_path %}
                                            <td class="status-cell{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</td>
                                        {% elif group.filter_type in ['inclusion', 'none'] %}
                                            {% for field_def in group.fields %}
                                                {% set path = group.json_path + '.' + field_def.key %}
                                                {% if field_def.get('render_type') == 'text_presence' %}
                                                    {% set text_val = data | get_json_path(path) %}
                                                    <td class="status-cell{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{{ '✔️' if text_val and (text_val|string).strip() else '❌' }}</td>
                                                {% else %}
                                                    <td class="status-cell{% if path in changed %} cell-changed{% endif %}">{{ data | get_json_path(path) | render_status }}</td>
                                                {% endif %}
                                            {% endfor %}
                                        {% endif %}
                                    {% endfor %}
                                    
                                    <td class="secondary-text-cell changed-cell">{{ entry.timestamp|format_changed_timestamp }}</td>
                                    <td class="status-cell changed-by-cell">{{ entry.model|render_changed_by }}</td>
                                    {% if entry.verification_data %}
                                        <td class="status-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.verified|render_status }}</td>
                                        <td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.estimated_score|default('') }}</td>
                                        <td class="status-cell">{{ entry.verification_data.verifier_model|render_verified_by }}</td>
                                    {% else %}
                                        <td class="status-cell">❔</td><td class="secondary-text-cell number-cell"></td><td class="status-cell">❔</td>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </tr>
                                {% endif %}
                                {% endfor %}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
            </div>
        </td>
        <td style="display:none" colspan="5"></td>
    </tr>
    {% endfor %}
</tbody>
{% endblock %}
```
